MoQ nightly benchmarks

Nightly 0513c0736, 0 flagged of 3339 series. Each series compares its newest point with the median of its previous 7. Red is worse and green is better by more than 10% and 3 times that window's median absolute deviation. Grey rows lack history or come from a source that did not finish tonight. Metrics and local reproduction

Paired comparison

Each source built the previous nightly's commit beside this one and ran both on one runner, alternating which went first. A quick screen flags a change beyond 10%; each slowdown then reruns in 3 alternating rounds of longer measurements, and fails the nightly when every round is slower and their median exceeds 10%. bench-rust: 4381af6f0...0513c0736; bench-rust-extra: 4381af6f0...0513c0736; bench-rust-shared: 4381af6f0...0513c0736; bench-rust-uring: 4381af6f0...0513c0736
